SOP extraction experiment

Geometry-refined Docling and PP-StructureV3 experiment. Synthetic fixtures only: 4 page designs in 16 paired variants. Includes native text/image recovery, ruled-grid reconstruction, connected-shape diagram grouping and token ownership. Rules target upright ruled pages; candidates still need review.

Region matches use one-to-one IoU ≥ 0.5. Table grid checks exclude ambiguous layout grids. Figure matching does not establish diagram semantics or correct cell ownership. Text flags are spatial diagnostics, not CER/WER. Unmatched elements and automatic flags require visual review.

EngineCompleted pagesTable regions matched / expectedBody figures matched / expectedDiagram regions matched / expectedComplete logical grids exact
docling16/1612/1616/168/88
paddle16/1612/1616/168/88

Blue=text, red=table, yellow=cell, purple=diagram/figure, green=ground-truth image. Timings include page preparation and parser work; startup/downloads are recorded separately. The measured run is sequential with pre-cached models; initialization, processing and exports are recorded separately in timing.json. This is a single run on the current CPU, not a hardware-independent benchmark.
